.

Case Case

For
p=\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n})
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(case)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}):\mathcal{E}

By induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{1}
, there exist types
\mathcal{M}_{1}
and derivations

\Pi_{1}^{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{1}

and
\Pi_{1}^{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1};x:\mathcal{M}_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
.
Similarly, by induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{2}
, there exist types
\mathcal{M}_{2}
and derivations

\Pi_{2}^{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{2}

and
\Pi_{2}^{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2};x:\mathcal{M}_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}:\mathcal{E}
.
Therfore, one can derive the following
\Pi_{\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n})}
.

\mathtt{(case)}

\Pi_{1}^{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1};x:\mathcal{M}_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
\Pi_{2}^{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2};x:\mathcal{M}_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}:\mathcal{E}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2};x:\mathcal{M}_{1}\cup\mathcal{M}_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}):\mathcal{E}

Being
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
closed, by
Lemma

A.4

\mathcal{M}=\mathcal{M}_{1}\cup\mathcal{M}_{2}
exists.
Moreover, by intersection lemma (
Lemma

4.2
) on
\Pi_{1}^{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
and

\Pi_{2}^{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
, one can derive

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{1}\cup\mathcal{M}_{2}
.

Case Return

For
p=\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w})
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{N}
\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{N}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

By induction hypothesis on
\Pi^{\prime}
, there exist types
\mathcal{M}
and derivations

\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}

and
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma;x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{N}
.
Therfore, one can derive the following
\Pi_{\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w})}
.

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma;x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{N}
\Gamma;x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{N}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

Thus, one can conclude this case with

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}=\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
.

Case Effect

p=\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(eff)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{N}

\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{N}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

and

Thus, one can derive the following
\Pi_{\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})}
.

\mathtt{(eff)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x:\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{N}

{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}

Case Handler

For
p=\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}

has the following form :

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}\Rightarrow\mathcal{F}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}

\Pi_{2}
, there exist types
\mathcal{M}_{2}
and derivations

\Pi_{2}^{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{2}

\Pi_{2}^{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2};x:\mathcal{M}_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
.
Therfore, one can derive the following
\Pi_{\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\Pi_{1}^{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1};x:\mathcal{M}_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{E}\Rightarrow\mathcal{F}
\Pi_{2}^{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2};x:\mathcal{M}_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}

Case Handler Branching

\mathtt{(hdlr)}

\Pi_{\sigma}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;y:\mathcal{N};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}

\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}

\mathtt{(hdlr)}

\Pi_{\sigma}^{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x:\mathcal{M}_{\sigma};y:\mathcal{N};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
(\Pi_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i},x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}

With
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}

.

∎

See
4.5

Proof.

By induction on the evaluation context.

•

If the context is of the form
\diamond
, then
\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>={\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
and

\mathcal{F}=\mathcal{E}
.
Thus, for every
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
such that
emptyset\vdash{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
,

\emptyset\vdash\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{E}
.

•

If the context is of the form
\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}
,
then the typing derivation is of the following form.

\mathtt{(let)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
\mathcal{E}^{\prime}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{E}
(\Pi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{E}

By induction hypothesis on
\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
, there is a type
\mathcal{F}

such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{F}

and for every
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
there is a derivation

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
.
We deduce the following derivation.

\mathtt{(let)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
\mathcal{E}^{\prime}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{E}
(\Pi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{E}

•

If the context is of the form
\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
,
then the typing derivation is of the following form.

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>:\mathcal{E}

By induction hypothesis on
\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
, there is a type
\mathcal{F}

such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{F}

and for every
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
there is a derivation

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
.
We deduce the following derivation.

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{E}

∎

Before proving Subject Reduction and Subject Expansion, we first establish the following two lemmas. The first states that any effect clause may be added to a handler, provided that it satisfies the
\#
relation. The second shows that this relation characterizes precisely the absence of clashes in the presence of handlers.

Lemma A.6 (
\mathsf{HEBI}
, Handler Weakening).

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
and
\psi
be either
\{\mathsf{return}(x)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}
or
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}
. If

\psi\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
,
then there exists

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\psi\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
.

Lemma A.7 (Clashes and Compatibility).

The term
\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})
, with
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\in{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
, is not a clash if and only if
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
. Similarly,
\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})
, with
\{\mathsf{return}(x)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\in{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
, is not a clash if and only if
\{\mathsf{return}(x)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
.

We split the proof of
Lemma

4.6
into separate proofs of Elementary Subject Reduction and Elementary Subject Expansion. Here, by elementary we refer to the fact that we consider only the basic reduction rules of
Fig.

1
. We can easily extend these results to the full reduction using
Lemma

4.5
, finally obtaining
Lemma

4.6
.

Lemma A.8 (
\mathsf{HEBI}
, Elementary Subject Expansion).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. If there exists

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}

then there exists

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
.

Proof.

By case analysis on the different rewrite rules:

Case
\mathtt{\beta}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{\beta}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
. Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is typable,
there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}

for some type
\mathcal{E}
. By anti-substitution lemma
(
Lemma

4.4
), since the free variables of
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}

are not bounded in
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
, there exist a type
\mathcal{M}
and type derivations

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
and

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}
.
Therefore, the following
\Pi^{\prime}
concludes this case :

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(abs)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{E}

Case
\mathtt{fix}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{fix}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
. Since

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is typable, there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

for some type
\mathcal{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Pi
is necessarily the
following form:

\mathtt{(app)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

By the anti-substitution lemma
(
Lemma

4.4
) with
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
, one deduces that
there exist a type
\mathcal{N}
and derivations

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{N}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}

and
\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{N}
.
Let
\Pi^{\prime}
be the following typing derivation, if
\mathcal{N}\neq\left\{\,\right\}
:

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(fixr)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{N}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}
\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{N}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

Otherwise, if
\mathcal{N}=\left\{\,\right\}
, then we can use the rule
\mathtt{(fixb)}

.

Case
\mathtt{case}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{case}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{case}(\underline{n};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m})
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}
with
0<n\leq m
.
Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is typable, there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}:\mathcal{E}

Case
\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ret}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}

\mathcal{E}

Case

Case
\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ret}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}

\mathcal{E}

4.4

\{\mathsf{return}(x)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}

\mathtt{(ret)}

.

By case analysis, we deduce that
\Pi_{r}
is necessarily of the following
form:

\mathtt{(abs)}

\left(\vrule height=16.21385pt,depth=1.94444pt,width=0.0pt\right.
\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 10.85696pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(hdl)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}}^{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}$}}\hbox{$\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}^{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}_{i}$}}}}\hbox{$y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{G}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{i\in I}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda y.\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}

is not a free variable of

, by context simplification
on

one can derive

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}}^{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}
.

Since

is not a clash, by
Lemma

A.7

\{\sigma(x;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
.
Let
\Pi^{\prime}
be the following derivation :

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\mathtt{(eff)}

(B)
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

(B)\;\coloneqq\;\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 36.79341pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(hdlr)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}}^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M};r:\left\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{E}$}}\hbox{$\begin{matrix}\{\sigma(x;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\\

One can conclude this case by noticing that

.

∎

Lemma A.9 (
\mathsf{HEBI}
, Elementary Subject Reduction).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}

is typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. If there
exists

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}

then there exists

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}
.

Proof.

By case analysis on the different rewrite rules:

Case
\mathtt{\beta}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto_{\mathtt{\beta}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
. Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is typable,
there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{E}

for some type
\mathcal{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Pi
is necessarily of the following
form:

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(abs)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{E}

By substitution lemma (
Lemma

4.3
)
with
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}
and
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
, there exists

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}
,
therefore concluding this case.

Case
\mathtt{fix}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto_{\mathtt{fix}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
. Since

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is typable, there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

for some type
\mathcal{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Pi
is necessarily in one of the two
following forms:

Base case:

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(fixb)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\left\{\,\right\}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

By typability of values (
Lemma

A.2
), one
has that

\Pi_{\left\{\,\right\}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\,\right\}
,
hence by the substitution lemma
(

4.3
) with
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}

and
\Pi_{\left\{\,\right\}}
, one deduces that there exists

. Let
\Pi^{\prime}
be the
following typing derivation:

\mathtt{(app)}

\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

One concludes this case by observing that

is typing

.

4.3
)
with
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}^{1}

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}

Case
\mathtt{case}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto_{\mathtt{\beta}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{case}(\underline{n};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m})
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}
with

.
Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is typable, there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}(\underline{n};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}):\mathcal{E}
for some type
\mathcal{E}
. By case analysis, we deduce that
\Pi
is
necessarily of the following form:

\mathtt{(case)}

\mathtt{(int)}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}:\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}:\mathcal{E}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}(\underline{n};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}):\mathcal{E}

One concludes this case by observing that
\Pi^{\prime}
is typing

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}

.

\mathcal{E}
. By

is necessarily of the following

\mathtt{(let)}

,

Case
\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ef}
.

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}

\mathcal{E}
. By

\Pi
is necessarily of the following

\mathtt{(let)}

(B)\;\coloneqq\;\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.90369pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(hdlr)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}}\mathrel{\triangleright}y:\mathcal{M};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}:\mathcal{E}$}}\hbox{$(\varphi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}$}}}}\hbox{$\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(x;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}:\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}

Proof.
