If
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}\rrbracket_{i\in I}
,
then
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})

where
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
and

\forall\;i\in I,\;\forall{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\rrbracket,\;\lambda x.\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\left\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\right\}\rrbracket
.
Therefore,
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})

and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}\rrbracket_{i\in I}
.

Conversly, if If
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}\rrbracket_{i\in I}
,
then
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})

where
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
and

\forall\;i\in I,\;\forall{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\rrbracket,\;\lambda x.\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\left\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\right\}\rrbracket
.
By unicity of the reduction,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})

and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}\rrbracket_{i\in I}
.
The case of
\llbracket\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\rrbracket
is treated
similarly.

∎

See
4.10

Proof.

By induction on
\Pi
. By case analysis on
p
, the
form of the
\Pi
can be deduced :

Case Integers

For
p=\underline{n}
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(int)}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}:\tau

Where
\tau\in\{\left\{\,\right\},\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}\}
.
In any case by definition,
\underline{n}\in\llbracket\tau\rrbracket
.

Case Variable

If
p=x
, then
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(var)}

x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}x:\mathcal{M}

For
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
,

x\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
, therfore

x\in\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}\vdash\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
.

Case Abstraction

For
p=\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(abs)}

\left(\Pi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}_{i}\right)_{i}
+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\rrbracket
,
by context reducibility (
Remark

4.8
)

\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{i}\rrbracket
.
By induction hypothesis on the
\Pi_{i}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}_{i}\rrbracket
, for some fixed
i\in I
.
By definition,

(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}){\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\rightarrow{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket
.
Thus by reducibility expansion (
Lemma

4.9
),

(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}){\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket
.
This being true for all
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{N}_{i}\rrbracket
,

\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket

and therefore
\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.

Case Fixpoint

For
p=\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
,
\Pi
has two possible forms :

Base case :

\mathtt{(fixb)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma;x:\left\{\,\right\}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}
\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}

Recursive case :

\mathtt{(fixr)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1};x:\mathcal{M}^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}^{\prime}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket
,
by context reducibility (
Remark

4.8
)

\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket\cap\llbracket\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket
.
By induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{1}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{\Gamma_{1}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}^{\prime}\vdash\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.
Since
\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\left\{\,\right\}\rrbracket

and
\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}:\left\{\,\right\}

(by typability of values
Lemma

A.2
),
we can assume to be in the recursive case with the induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{2}

asserting
\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}^{\prime}\rrbracket

(where
\mathcal{M}^{\prime}
is possibly empty).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}_{i}\rrbracket
, for some fixed
i\in I
.
By Definition

(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}){\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}^{\prime}\rightarrow{}{}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}\{x\backslash(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\}){\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket
.
Thus by reducibility expansion (
Lemma

4.9
),

(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}){\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket
.
This being true for all
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}_{i}\rrbracket
,

\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket

and therefore
\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\vdash\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.

Case Let-Binding

In order to prove this case, let us prove a small lemma before.

Lemma A.10.

The following proprety
(P)
is true for all
\mathcal{E},\mathcal{F},(\mathcal{M}_{i},\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}

such that
\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
.

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket,\forall{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\bigcap\limits_{i\in I}\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{G}_{i}\rrbracket,\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\mathcal{F}\rrbracket.

Proof.

By induction on
\varphi\mathrel{\triangleright}\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
.

•

Base case.
Let us assume
\varphi
has the following form.

\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\rightarrow}\mathcal{F}

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\rrbracket
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}\vdash\mathcal{F}\rrbracket
.
There exists a value
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket

\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\rightarrow{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{F}\rrbracket
.
By reducibility expansion (
Lemma

4.9
),
one can conclude
\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\mathcal{F}\rrbracket
.

•

Recursive case.
Let us assume
\varphi
has the following form.

\left(\vrule height=18.52078pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.
\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.47906pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\enskip\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\varphi_{i}$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to10.33325pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\mathcal{E}_{i}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{k}^{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{k\in K_{i}}\;}}\mathcal{F}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{i\in I}
\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{k}^{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{k\in K_{i},i\in I}\;}}\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\bigcap_{k\in K_{i},i\in I}\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\vdash\mathcal{G}_{k}^{i}\rrbracket
.
By definition, there exists a value
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket

and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket

such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s})
.
Therefore
\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\rightarrow{}{}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})
.

Let
i\in I
and
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\in\llbracket\mathcal{N}_{i}\rrbracket
.
Since
y
is a free variable of
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
,

(\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\{y\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\}=\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\{y\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
.
Therfore by induction hypothesis with
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\{y\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\bigcap_{k\in K_{i}}\llbracket x:\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\vdash\mathcal{G}_{k}^{i}\rrbracket
,

\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\{y\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\mathcal{F}_{i}\rrbracket
.
This being true for all
i\in I
and
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\in\llbracket\mathcal{N}_{i}\rrbracket
,

\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{F}_{i}\rrbracket
.
And since
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
, we can deduce

\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.

Therfore by reducibility expansion (
Lemma

4.9
),
one can conclude
\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.

∎

Let us now use this lemma to prove the following case.
For
p=\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(let)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
\varphi

.

{\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}}

(\Pi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}
\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}

By induction hypothesis on the
\Pi_{i}
(resp. on
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}}
),

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket\Gamma_{i},x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{G}_{i}\rrbracket

(resp.
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket\Delta\vdash\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
). Let

\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\rrbracket
,
by the previous lemma (
Lemma

A.10
) on

\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
,

\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{\Delta\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{F}\rrbracket
.
Therefore,
\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\mathcal{F}\rrbracket
.

Case Application

For
p={\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{1}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{2}
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(app)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{1}:\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{2}:\mathcal{M}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{1}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{2}:\mathcal{E}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket
.
By induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{1}
(resp. on
\Pi_{2}
),

{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{1}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{1}\{\Gamma_{1}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}\rrbracket

(resp.
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{2}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{2}\{\Gamma_{2}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
).
By definition
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{1}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{2}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket

and so
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{1}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}_{2}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\vdash\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.

Case Case

For
p=\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n})
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(case)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}:\mathcal{E}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}):\mathcal{E}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket
,
by context reducibility (
Remark

4.8
)

\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}\rrbracket\cap\llbracket\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket
.
By induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{1}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{\Gamma_{1}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathsf{m}\rrbracket=\{\underline{m}\}
,
and similarly by induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{2}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}\{\Gamma_{2}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.
We also define
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{k}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{k}\{\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}

for all
k\leq n
.
Therfore,

\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}^{\prime})=\mathsf{case}(\underline{m};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}^{\prime})\rightarrow{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.
Thus by reducibility expansion (
Lemma

4.9
),

\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}^{\prime})\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
,
and so
\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n})\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\vdash\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.

Case Return

For
p=\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket
,
by induction hypothesis on
\Pi^{\prime}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{\Gamma\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
.
Therfore,
\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\{\Gamma\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\rrbracket

and so
\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\in\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\rrbracket
.

Case Effect

For
p=\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(eff)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
(\Pi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}_{i})_{i\in I}
\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\rrbracket

and let
i\in I
.
By context reducibility (
Remark

4.8
)

\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{i}\rrbracket

and
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket
.
By induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{i}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{\Gamma_{i}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{N}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket
,
similarly by induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{\Delta\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
.
This being true for all
i\in I
,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{E}_{i}\rrbracket

and therefore

\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}^{\prime};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime})\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.
Thus,
\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})\in\llbracket\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.

Case Handle

For
p=\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{E}\Rightarrow\mathcal{F}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{F}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket
,
by context reducibility (
Remark

4.8
)

\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}\rrbracket\cap\llbracket\Gamma_{2}\rrbracket
.
By induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{1}
(resp. on
\Pi_{2}
),

{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\{\Gamma_{1}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\Rightarrow\mathcal{F}\rrbracket

(resp.
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{\Gamma_{2}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
).
Therfore,
\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\mathcal{F}\rrbracket

and so
\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\llbracket\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\vdash\mathcal{F}\rrbracket
.

Case Handler Return

For
p=\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
,
\Pi
has the following form:

\mathtt{(hdlr_{ret})}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma;y:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket
. By induction hypothesis on
\Pi^{\prime}
,
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{\Gamma\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\vdash\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
. Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\in\llbracket\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\rrbracket
, there exists
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})
. Therefore,
\mathsf{with}\,\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\mathsf{with}\,\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\rightarrow{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\{y\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
. By
Lemma

4.9
,
\mathsf{with}\,\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
. This being true for all
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\in\llbracket\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\rrbracket
,
\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\}\in\llbracket\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
and therefore
\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\}\in\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.

Case Handler

For
p=\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
,
\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(hdlr)}

\Pi_{\sigma}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;y:\mathcal{M};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
\begin{matrix}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\\
(\Pi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}\end{matrix}
\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}

Let
\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\in\llbracket\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\rrbracket=\llbracket\Delta\rrbracket\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket\Gamma_{i}\rrbracket
.
By induction hypothesis on the
\Pi_{i}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}:={\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\{+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\rrbracket
.
Similarly, by induction hypothesis on
\Pi_{\sigma}
,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\{\Delta\backslash\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\}\in\llbracket y:\mathcal{M};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\vdash\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.
Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.
There exists
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\llbracket\mathcal{M}\rrbracket
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket x:\mathcal{N}_{i}\vdash\mathcal{F}_{i}\rrbracket

such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})
.
Therefore
\mathsf{with}\,(\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime})\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\mathsf{with}\,(\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime})\,\mathsf{handle}\,\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\rightarrow{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\{y\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\{r\backslash\lambda x.\mathsf{with}\,(\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime})\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}
.

Let us show that
\lambda x.\mathsf{with}\,(\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime})\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.
For
i\in I
, by handler weakening (
Lemma

A.6
)
and substitution lemma (
Lemma

4.3
) on
\Pi_{i}
,
there exists
\Pi_{i}^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}
.
Since each derivation
\Pi_{i}^{\prime}
is smaller than the
derivation
\Pi_{i}
, we can apply the induction hypothesis on the

\Pi_{i}^{\prime}
and have
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}\in\bigcap_{i\in I}\llbracket\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\rrbracket

Therefore, by definition of
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
,
\lambda x.\mathsf{with}\,(\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime})\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\llbracket\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
.

Thus
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\{y\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\{r\backslash\lambda x.\mathsf{with}\,(\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime})\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket

and by reducibility expansion (
Lemma

4.9
),

\mathsf{with}\,(\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime})\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\in\llbracket\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.
This being true for all
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\rrbracket
,

\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}\rrbracket

and therefore
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\in\llbracket\Delta+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}\rrbracket
.

∎

Appendix B
Proofs of Section
5

B.1.
Section
5.1

The aim of this section is to prove Subject Reduction for
\mathsf{HEB}
. Since the arguments are very similar to those already used in
Section

A.1
, we only present in detail the proof of
Theorem

B.3
.

Lemma B.1 (
\mathsf{HEB}
Substitution Lemma).

Let
p\in\Lambda
and
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\texttt{Val}}}
such that

\Pi_{p}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x:\mathsf{M}\vdash p::\mathsf{T}
and

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
.
Then, there exists

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\vdash p\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\mathsf{T}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Pi_{p}
.
∎

Lemma B.2 (
\mathsf{HEB}
Handler Weakening).

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathsf{F}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}
. If

\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
,
then there exists a derivation, that we call
\Pi^{(\sigma)}
, such that

\Pi^{(\sigma)}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathsf{F}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Pi
.
∎

Theorem B.3 (
\mathsf{HEB}
Elementary Subject Reduction).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
,
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
then it exists
\Pi^{\prime}
such that
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}::\mathsf{E}
.

Proof.

By case analysis on the different rewrite rules:

Case
\mathtt{\beta}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto_{\mathtt{\beta}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
. Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is typable,
there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{E}

for some type
\mathsf{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Pi
is necessarily of the following
form:

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(abs)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{E}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\left\{\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\right\}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{E}

By substitution lemma (
Lemma

B.1
)
with
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}
and
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
, there exists

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\mathsf{E}
,
therefore concluding this case.

Case
\mathtt{fix}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto_{\mathtt{fix}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
. Since

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is typable, there exists a derivation

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{E}

for some type
\mathsf{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Pi
is necessarily in the
following form:

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(fix)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}x::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}

\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{E}

By the substitution lemma (
Lemma

B.1
)
with
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}
, there exist a derivation

\Pi^{\prime}_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
. Let
\Pi^{\prime}
be the following
typing derivation:

\mathtt{(app)}

\Pi^{\prime}_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{E}

Case
\mathtt{case}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is
typed and

Lemma B.4 (
\mathsf{HEB}
Context Typing).

\mathsf{HEB}

B.2.
Section
5.3

In this section, we prove Relative Subject Expansion for refinements between
\mathsf{HEBI}
and
\mathsf{HEB}
type derivations. We begin by stating two lemmas showing that the notion of refinement between types is well-behaved with respect to inclusion, both for individual value types and for contexts.

Lemma B.5 (Subset Refinement).

Let
\mathsf{M}
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
value type and
\mathcal{M}
an
\mathsf{HEBI}
value type such that
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
. If
\mathcal{N}\subseteq\mathcal{M}
then
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{N}
.

Corollary B.6 (Context Refinement).

Let
\Delta
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
types context and
\Gamma
be an
\mathsf{HEBI}
types one such that
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.

•

Let
\mathsf{M}
and
\mathcal{M}
be two types such that
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
, then
\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\Gamma;x:\mathcal{M}
.

•

For each
\Gamma^{\prime}\subseteq\Gamma
we have
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma^{\prime}
.

In
Fig.

15
, we present the missing refinement rules between
\mathsf{HEBI}
and
\mathsf{HEB}
derivations that were not included in
Fig.

10
. Moreover, in
Fig.

16
, we introduce the reduction rules between derivations that are missing in
Fig.

11
. We then state several properties of this refinement system that will be useful in the proof of
Lemma

B.11
.
