y.\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{G}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\end{array}\end{array}

Figure 16.
Reductions between two
\mathsf{HEB}
derivations, cont’d.

Lemma B.7 (Conlcusion Refinement).

If
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\vdash p::\mathsf{U}\sqsubset\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\vdash p:\mathcal{E}
, then
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
and
\mathsf{U}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}
.

Proposition B.8 (Weakening Refinement).

Let
p
be a term,
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta_{1}\vdash p::\mathsf{U}
be its derivation and
\Delta_{2}
be a context such that for each
x::\mathsf{M}\in\Delta_{1}
and
x::\mathsf{N}\in\Delta_{2}
then
\mathsf{M}=\mathsf{N}
. Let
\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\vdash p:\mathcal{E}
be an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type derivation, then
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
if and only if
\Pi^{(\Delta_{2})}\sqsubset\Xi
.

Lemma B.9 (
\mathsf{HEB}
, Refinement Handler Elimination).

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}::\mathsf{F}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}
be a derivation,
\psi
be either
\{\mathsf{return}(x)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}
or
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}
. If
\psi\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
and it exists an
\mathsf{HEBI}
derivation
\Xi
such that
\Pi^{(\psi)}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\cup\psi::\mathsf{F}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\cup\psi:\mathcal{F}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
, then it exists a derivation
\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Lemma B.10 (
\mathsf{HEB}
Refinement Anti-substitution Lemma).

Let
p\in\Lambda

be any term,
x
a variable occourring free in
p
and
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\texttt{Val}}}
a closed value.
If
\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\vdash p\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\tau
for some
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\tau
and there exists an
\mathsf{HEB}
type
\mathsf{M}
such that
\Pi_{p}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\vdash p::\mathsf{T}
,
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\vdash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
and
\Pi_{p}\{x\backslash\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\sqsubset\Pi
, then it exists an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\mathcal{M}
such that

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
and

\Pi_{p}\sqsubset\Xi_{p}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma;x:\mathcal{M}\vdash p:\tau
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Xi
.
∎

Lemma B.11 (
\mathsf{HEB}
Relative Elementary Subject Expansion).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
be two closed term such that

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. If there exists

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}
and
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
such that
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
and also there exist
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
such that
\Pi\rightsquigarrow\Pi^{\prime}
,
then there exists

\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Proof.

By case analysis on the different rewrite rules.

Case
\mathtt{\beta}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{\beta}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
. By hypothesis,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is typable with a
\mathsf{HEB}
type derivation
\Pi
that, by case analysis, has to have the following shape.

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(abs)}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{U}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{U}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{U}

Also by hypothesis, there exist two derivations

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}
and
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\mathsf{U}
such that
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
.
Since by hypothesis
\Pi\rightsquigarrow\Pi^{\prime}
, by definition of
\rightsquigarrow
is clear that
\Pi^{\prime}=\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
.
We can then apply anti-substitution lemma
(
Lemma

B.10
), obtaining that there exist a type
\mathcal{M}
and type derivations

\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
and

\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}
, where
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
.
Therefore, we can construct the following
\Xi^{\prime}
:

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(abs)}

\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}

\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{E}

Finally, is easy to see that
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
.

Case
\mathtt{fix}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{fix}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
.

Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is simply typable,
\Pi
has the following shape.

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(fix)}

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}x::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{U}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{U}
\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{U}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}){\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{U}

Since

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is typable, there exists a derivation

\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

for some refinement type
\mathcal{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Xi^{\prime}
is necessarily the
following form:

\mathtt{(app)}

\Xi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}
\Xi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

Also,
\Pi^{\prime}
has to have the following shape

\mathtt{(app)}

\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{U}
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{U}

Since by hypothesis
\Pi\rightsquigarrow\Pi^{\prime}
, by definition of
\rightsquigarrow
we also have that
\Pi^{\prime}_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}=\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\{x\backslash\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\}
and
\Pi^{\prime}_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}
.

We can then apply the anti-substitution lemma
(
Lemma

B.10
) on
\Pi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}
.
One then deduces that
there exist a refinement type
\mathcal{N}
and derivations

\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{N}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}

and
\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{N}
,
where
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\sqsubset\Xi_{\mathsf{fix}}
.
Let
\Xi
be the following typing derivation, if
\mathcal{N}\neq\left\{\,\right\}
:

\mathtt{(app)}

\mathtt{(fixr)}

\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{N}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}
\Xi_{\mathsf{fix}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{N}
\Xi^{1}_{\mathsf{fix}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}

\Xi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}(\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

Notice how by definition of the
\sqsubset
on the fix rule, we obtain that

\Pi_{\mathsf{fix}}\sqsubset\Xi^{1}_{\mathsf{fix}}
, so finally
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.
Otherwise, if
\mathcal{N}=\left\{\,\right\}
, we can conlude similarly, by usinf the rule
\mathtt{(fixb)}

.

Case
\mathtt{case}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{case}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{case}(\underline{n};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m})
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}
with
0<n\leq m
.
Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is simply typable,
\Pi
has the following shape.

\mathtt{(case)}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}::\mathsf{m}
(\Pi_{k}\triangleright\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{k}::\mathsf{U})_{0<k\leq m}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m})::\mathsf{U}

Since
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is typable, we have
\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}:\mathcal{E}

for some type
\mathcal{E}
. Furthermore, it exists a
\Pi^{\prime}
such that
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}::\mathsf{U}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
and
\Pi\rightsquigarrow\Pi^{\prime}
.
Let
\Xi
be the following typing derivation:

\mathtt{(case)}

\mathtt{(int)}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}:\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}

\Pi_{n}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}:\mathcal{E}
0<n\leq m
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}(\underline{n};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}):\mathcal{E}

It is clear that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case
\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ret}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ret}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By
definition,
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
. The derivation
\Pi
is in the following form:

\mathtt{(let)}

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathsf{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})::\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{F}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\rightarrow}\mathsf{F}

\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathsf{F}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathsf{F}

Also,
there exist derivations
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\mathsf{F}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{F}
.
Since by hypothesis
\Pi\rightsquigarrow\Pi^{\prime}
, by definition of
\rightsquigarrow
we have that
\Pi^{\prime}=\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
.
By anti-substitution lemma (
Lemma

B.10
), there exist a refinement type
\mathcal{M}

and derivations
\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}

and
\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
,
where
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
.
Thus, let
\Xi
be the following derivation :

\mathtt{(let)}

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\rightarrow}\mathcal{F}

\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{F}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}

Finally, we can see that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case
\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ef}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ef}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By
definition,
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})
. There exists a derivation

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}):\mathcal{E}

for some type
\mathcal{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Xi^{\prime}
is necessarily of the following
form:

\mathtt{(eff)}

\left(\vrule height=7.5pt,depth=1.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.
\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}$}}\hbox{$A_{i}$}}}$}\right)_{i\in I}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

(A_{i})\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(let)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 19.58656pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=8.26085pt,depth=1.94444pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi^{i}_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}}\mathrel{\triangleright}y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{G}_{i}$}}\hbox{$B_{i}$}}}$}\right)_{i\in I}$}}}\hbox{$(\Xi_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}_{k}^{i})_{k\in K_{i}}$}}}}}\hbox{$y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}_{i}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

(B_{i})\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.47906pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\enskip\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\varphi_{i}$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to10.33325pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\mathcal{G}_{i}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{k}^{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{k\in K_{i}}\;}}\mathcal{E}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Since

is not a free variable of
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
, by context simplification (
Remark

A.1
) on each
\Xi_{k}^{i}
one can derive
\Xi_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}_{k}^{i}
.
Furthermore, it exists a
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})::\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\}\!\!\}
.
Let
\Xi
be the following typing derivation:

\mathtt{(let)}

(A)
(B)
(\Xi_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}_{k}^{i})_{k\in K_{i},i\in I}

(A)\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 22.21933pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(eff)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}$}}\hbox{$(\Xi^{i}_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}}\mathrel{\triangleright}y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}$}}}}\hbox{$\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

By looking at the definition of this reduction case in
Figure

16
, from
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
we obtain easily that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
. In particular, notice that the subderivation
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
of
\Pi^{\prime}
is such that
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}^{(y::\mathsf{N})}\sqsubset\Xi^{i}_{k}
for each
i\in I
,
k\in K
. By
Proposition

B.8
, we obtain
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\sqsubset\Xi^{i}_{k}
.

Case
\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ret}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ret}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By
definition,
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
with

{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}=\{\mathsf{return}(x)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}^{\prime}

Since

has the following form.

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\mathtt{(ret)}

There exist two derivations

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{E}
and

\mathtt{(ret)}

.

Case
\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ef}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ef}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
.

By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\{r\backslash\lambda y.\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}

with

In order to prove
Lemma

5.3
, it only remains to prove the following lemma.

Lemma B.12 (
\mathsf{HEB}
Refinement Context Typing).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
and

\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
a computation context such that

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>::\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>:\mathcal{E}
.
Then there exists an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\mathcal{F}
and an
\mathsf{HEB}
type
\mathsf{F}

such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{F}\sqsubset\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathcal{F}
and
for every
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathsf{F}\sqsubset\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
, then there exist
\Pi^{\prime}
and
\Xi^{\prime}
such that

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{E}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
.
∎

B.3.
Section
5.4

We start this section giving some preliminary definitions and properties needed in order to prove the correctness and computability of
\mathcal{A}
.
Let
\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
be typable in
\mathsf{HEBI}
. By looking at the corresponding typing rule, we can notice that this implies the existence of a certain number
n
of types
\mathcal{N}_{i}
and derivations
\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{N}_{i+1}
such that
\mathcal{N}_{0}=\left\{\,\right\}
,
\mathcal{N}_{n}=\mathcal{M}
and
\Gamma=+_{i\in n}\Gamma_{i}
. We can see this sequence of derivations as a path in the graph
\mathcal{G}(\Pi,\Gamma,\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E})
, with the additional requirements that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi_{i}
and
\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{N}_{i}
for each
i\leq n
. In
Lemma

B.15
, we prove that this path is simple, therefore the length of such sequences of derivations is bounded by the size of the graph itself.

Definition B.12.

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x:\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
type derivation and
\Gamma
be an
\mathsf{HEBI}
typing context. We define a graph
\mathcal{G}(\Pi,\Gamma,\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E})
having as nodes couples
(\Gamma^{\prime},\mathcal{M})
, where
\Gamma^{\prime}\subseteq\Gamma
and
\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
. There exist an arrow between two nodes
(\Gamma_{1},\mathcal{M}_{1})
and
(\Gamma_{2},\mathcal{M}_{2})
if and only if it exists an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type derivation
\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x:\mathcal{M}_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{2}
such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
with
\Gamma_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\subseteq\Gamma
and
\Gamma_{2}=\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
.

Remark B.13 (Finite Fixpoint).

The size of
\mathcal{G}(\Pi,\Gamma,\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E})
is bounded by
|Sub(\Gamma)|\cdot|\mathtt{Refn}(\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E})|
.

Remark B.14.

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta_{s};x:\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
type derivation and
\Gamma
be an
\mathsf{HEBI}
typing context. If there exists a path
\phi
between two nodes
(\Delta,\mathcal{N})
and
(\Delta^{\prime},\mathcal{N}^{\prime})
in
\mathcal{G}(\Pi,\Gamma,\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E})
, then there exist some
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}
for
i\leq|\phi|+1
,
\Gamma_{i}\subseteq\Gamma
and some derivations
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{i+1}
for
i\leq|\phi|
such that
\Delta=\Gamma_{0}
and
\Delta^{\prime}=+_{i\leq n}\Gamma_{i}
,
\mathcal{N}=\mathcal{M}_{0}
and
\mathcal{N}^{\prime}=\mathcal{M}_{|\phi|+1}
.

Lemma B.15 (Fixpoint Path).

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
type derivation,
\Pi^{\prime}
its premise and
\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
, it exists a
\Gamma_{0}\subseteq\Gamma
and a simple path
\phi
from
(\Gamma_{0},\left\{\,\right\})
to
(\Gamma,\mathcal{M})
in
\mathcal{G}(\Pi^{\prime},\Gamma,\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E})
.

This lemma can be seen as a sort of dual of
Lemma

B.15
: if we have a sequence of
\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{i+1}
for some suitables
\mathcal{M}_{i}
, we can merge them in a derivation of
\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
.

Lemma B.16.

Let
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
type derivation,
n
an integer,
\mathcal{M}_{i}
with
i\leq n
be
\mathsf{HEBI}
types such that
\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}
and
\mathcal{M}_{0}=\left\{\,\right\}
. Furthermore, let
\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{i+1}
with
i\leq n-1
be a sequence of
\mathsf{HEBI}
type derivations such that
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}
. Then, there exist
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
and
\Omega\mathrel{\triangleright}+_{i\leq n-1}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{n}
such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Omega
.

As we said in the discussion about
\mathsf{HEBI}
computational types, they can be seen as trees.
The following measure counts the number of leaves in such trees.

Definition B.16.

We define a measure on computation
\mathsf{HEBI}
types.

\begin{array}[]{lcr}|\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}|=1&&|\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}|=\sum_{i\in I}|\mathcal{E}_{i}|\end{array}

Remark B.17.

The measure
|\mathcal{F}|
is finite for each
\mathcal{F}
, since the set
I
is finite.

The Leaf Replacement relation
\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
states that the type
\mathcal{F}
is created from
\mathcal{E}
by substituting each type
\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}_{i}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
in a leaf of
\mathcal{E}
, with the corresponding type
\mathcal{G}_{i}
. This imples that the size of
I
is smaller then the number of leaves of
\mathcal{E}
(
i.e.

|I|\leq|\mathcal{E}|
). Moreover
|\mathcal{E}|\leq|\mathcal{F}|
, since
|\mathcal{G}_{i}|\geq 1
.

Lemma B.18 (Finite Leaf Replacement).

If
\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
then
|I|\leq|\mathcal{F}|
.

We now define a new formula
\mathcal{B}
, in the same way as we did for
\mathcal{A}
. This formula checks whether some given types satisfy the Leaf Replacement relation.

Definition B.18.

Let
I
be a set,
\mathcal{E}
,
\mathcal{F}
,
\mathcal{M}_{i}
and
\mathcal{G}_{i}
for each
i\in I
be
\mathsf{HEBI}
types. We define a formula
\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|},\mathcal{F})
that checks if the relation
\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
holds. If it does, we write
\models\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|},\mathcal{F})
. We describe this formula in
Fig.

17
by induction on
\mathcal{E}
.

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathcal{B}\Biggl({\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}},{\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|}},{\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|}},{\mathcal{F}}\Biggl)&=&(|I|=1)\wedge(\mathcal{M}=\mathcal{M}_{1})\wedge(\mathcal{F}=\mathcal{G}_{1})\\[14.22636pt]
\mathcal{B}\Biggl({\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{j}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{j}\}\!\!\}_{j\in J}},{\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|}},{\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|}},{\mathcal{F}}\Biggl)&=&\begin{array}[]{c}\mathcal{F}=\prescript{\sigma^{\prime}[\mathcal{M}^{\prime}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{k}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}^{\prime}_{k}\}\!\!\}_{k\in K}\wedge\\
(\sigma=\sigma^{\prime})\wedge\\
(|J|=|K|)\wedge(\mathcal{M}=\mathcal{M}^{\prime})\wedge\\
\forall j\in J.(\mathcal{N}_{j}=\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{j})\wedge\\
\forall j\in J.\,\exists K_{j}\subseteq I.I=\bigcup\limits_{j\in J}K_{j}\\
\bigwedge_{j\in J}\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E}^{\prime}_{j},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|K_{j}|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|K_{j}|},\mathcal{F}^{\prime}_{j})\\
\end{array}\\[14.22636pt]
\end{array}

Figure 17.
Definition of
\mathcal{B}

Proposition B.19 (Correctness of
\mathcal{B}
).

Let
I
be a set,
\mathcal{E}
,
\mathcal{F}
,
\mathcal{M}_{i}
and
\mathcal{G}_{i}
for each
i\in I
be
\mathsf{HEBI}
types. It holds that
\models\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|},\mathcal{F})
if and only if
\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
.

Lemma B.20 (Computability of
\mathcal{B}
).

The function
\models\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|},\mathcal{F})
is computable for each
\mathcal{E}
,
\mathcal{F}
,
\mathcal{M}_{i}
and
\mathcal{G}_{i}
with
i\in I
.

We can now finally prove the correctness of
\mathcal{A}
. In
Fig.

18
are described the cases of
\mathcal{A}
that were not included in
Fig.

12
.
We procede by splitting the proof of
Proposition

5.5
into two separate proofs of soundness and completeness.

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathcal{A}\Biggl(\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hskip 17.68762pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\dots$}}\hbox{$\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{G}$}}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{F}$}}}\par}\par}}}$},\\[14.22636pt]
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,y\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}\end{array}\Biggl)&=&\begin{array}[]{c}\exists\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}.\,\exists I.\,|I|\leq|\mathcal{F}|\\
\wedge\forall i\in I.\,\exists\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}.\,\exists\mathsf{G}\sqsubset\mathcal{G}_{i}.\\
\exists\Gamma^{\prime},\Gamma_{i}\subseteq\Gamma.\,(\Gamma=\Gamma^{\prime}+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i})\\
\land\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E})\\
\bigwedge_{i\in I}\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{G}_{i})\\
\wedge\,\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|},\mathcal{F})\\
\end{array}\\[48.36958pt]
\mathcal{A}\Biggl(\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hskip 3.98329pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{n}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$(\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{i}::\mathsf{E})_{0<i\leq n}$}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n})::\mathsf{E}$}}}\par}\par}}}$},\\[14.22636pt]
\qquad\qquad\qquad\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}):\mathcal{E}\end{array}\Biggl)&=&\begin{array}[]{c}\exists\mathsf{n}\sqsubset\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}.\,\exists\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\subseteq\Gamma.\\
(\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2})\\
\land\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{m\right\})\\
\wedge\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}:\mathcal{E})\\
\end{array}\\[28.45274pt]
\mathcal{A}\Biggl(\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 42.21684pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})::\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}$}}\par}\par}}}$},\\
\qquad\qquad\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\end{array}\Biggl)&=&\begin{array}[]{c}\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M})\end{array}\\[28.45274pt]
\mathcal{A}\Biggl(\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7315pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{N}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}$}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})::\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\}\!\!\}$}}\par}\par}}}$},\\[14.22636pt]
\qquad\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\end{array}\Biggl)&=&\begin{array}[]{c}\forall i\in I.\,\exists\Gamma^{\prime},\Gamma_{i}\subseteq\Gamma.\\
(\Gamma=\Gamma^{\prime}+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i})\\
\land\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M})\\
\bigwedge_{i\in I}\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}_{i})\\
\end{array}\\[28.45274pt]
\mathcal{A}\Biggl(\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 45.82803pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;y::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}::\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}$}}\par}\par}}}$},\\[14.22636pt]
\quad\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}:\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}\end{array}\Biggl)&=&\begin{array}[]{c}\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},\Gamma;y:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E})\end{array}\\[28.45274pt]
\end{array}

Figure 18.
Description of the formula
\mathcal{A}
, cont’d.

Proposition B.21 (Soundness of
\mathcal{A}
).

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\vdash p::\mathsf{T}
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
derivation,
J_{s}\coloneq\Delta\vdash p::\mathsf{T}
its conclusion and
J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\vdash p:\tau
an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type judgement such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}
. If
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
then it exists an
\mathsf{HEBI}
derivation
\Xi
, with conclusion
J_{r}
, such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Pi
.

Case Integers

\Pi
has the following form:

\mathtt{(int)}

0<n\leq m
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}::\mathsf{m}

We have
J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}:\mathcal{M}
with
\mathcal{M}\in\left\{\left\{\,\right\},\left\{\mathsf{i}\right\}\right\}
. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, we have that
\Gamma=\emptyset
and either
\mathcal{M}=\left\{\,\right\}
or
i=n
. We can construct the derivation
\Xi
as follows:

\mathtt{(var)}

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}:\mathcal{M}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Variable

\Pi
has the following form:

\mathtt{(var)}

\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}x::\mathsf{M}

We have
J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma;x:\mathcal{M}^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}x:\mathcal{M}
, and since
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}
, we have that
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
and
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}^{\prime}
. Moreover, since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, then
\Gamma=\emptyset
and
\mathcal{M}=\mathcal{M}^{\prime}
.
We then can construct the derivation
\Xi
as follows:

\mathtt{(var)}

x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}x:\mathcal{M}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Abstraction

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(abs)}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}_{i\in I}
, therefore
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
,
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}
and
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}_{i}
for each
i\in I
. Let
J^{\prime}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi^{\prime}
.
Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exist
\Gamma_{i}\subseteq\Gamma
such that
\Gamma=+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}
. We set
J^{i}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}_{i}
and have that
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi^{\prime},J^{i}_{r})
for each
i\in I
. Notice also that
J^{\prime}_{s}\sqsubset J^{i}_{r}
, therefore, by induction hypothesis, we have a derivations
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}\triangleright J^{i}_{r}
for each
i\in I
. We can then construct the following derivation :

\mathtt{(abs)}

(\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}_{i})_{i\in I}
+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}_{i\in I}

Finally, we have
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Fixpoint

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(fix)}

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}_{i\in I}
, so
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
,
\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}
. Let
J^{\prime}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi^{\prime}
. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exists an integer
n
, some contexts
\Gamma_{j}\subseteq\Gamma
for
j\leq n
such that
\Gamma=+_{j\leq n}\Gamma_{j}
and some
\mathsf{HEBI}
types
