\mathcal{M}_{j}
for
j\leq n+1
such that
\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}
,
\mathcal{M}_{0}=\left\{\,\right\}
and
\mathcal{M}_{n+1}=\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}_{i\in I}
. We set
J^{j}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{j};x:\mathcal{M}_{j}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{j+1}
and have that
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{j}_{r})
for each
j\leq n
. Notice also that
J^{\prime}_{s}\sqsubset J^{j}_{r}
, therefore, by induction hypothesis, we obtain derivations
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi_{j}\triangleright J^{j}_{r}
for each
j\leq n
.
By
Lemma

B.16
it exists a derivation
\Omega\mathrel{\triangleright}+_{0\leq j\leq n}\Gamma_{j}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}_{n+1}
and
\Pi\sqsubset\Omega
.

Case Application

\Pi
has the following form:

\mathtt{(app)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{M}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{E}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}
, so
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
. Let
J^{1}_{s}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusions respectively of
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{2}
. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exists two context
\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\subseteq\Gamma
such that
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}
and an
\mathcal{M}
such that
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
. We set
J^{1}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}
and
J^{1}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
, having
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{2}_{r})
.
By
Corollary

B.6
we obtain
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{2}_{r}
. Therefore, by induction hypothesis, we have derivations
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi_{1}\triangleright J^{1}_{r}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{2}\triangleright J^{2}_{r}
.
We can then construct
\Xi
as follows:

\mathtt{(app)}

\Xi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}
\Xi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Let-Binding

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(let)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
\mathsf{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{G}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\rightarrow}\mathsf{F}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{G}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{F}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,y\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
, so
\mathsf{F}\sqsubset\mathcal{F}
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.
We start by finding two contexts
\Gamma^{\prime}
and
\Gamma_{i}
for each
i\in I
such that
\Gamma=\Gamma^{\prime}+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}
.
Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exist a set
I
, contexts
\Gamma^{\prime},\Gamma_{i}\subseteq\Gamma
such that
\Gamma=_{+}{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}
and
\mathsf{HEBI}
types
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}
,
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}
and
\mathsf{G}\sqsubset\mathcal{G}_{i}
.
We set
J^{\prime}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
and
J^{i}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{G}_{i}
and have
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{\prime}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{i}_{r})
for each
i\in I
.
Notice also that, by
Corollary

B.6
,
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{\prime}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{i}_{r}
.
Therefore, by induction hypothesis we have derivations
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}\triangleright J^{\prime}_{r}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}\triangleright J^{i}_{r}
for each
i\in I
.
Furthermore,
\models\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|I|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|I|},\mathcal{F})
, so by
Proposition

B.19
the relation
\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
holds.
Thus, one can derive the following
\Xi
.

\mathtt{(let)}

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{i\in I}\;}}\mathcal{F}
(\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}
\Gamma+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,y\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}

Finally, obtaining that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Case

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(case)}

\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{n}
(\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{i}::\mathsf{E})_{0<i\leq n}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n})::\mathsf{E}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}):\mathcal{E}
, so
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.
Let
J^{1}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{1}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{2}
. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exists two contexts
\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\subseteq\Gamma
such that
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}
and an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
such that
\mathsf{n}\sqsubset\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
.
We set
J^{1}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{m\right\}
and
J^{2}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}:\mathcal{E}
, having
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{2}_{r})
.
By
Corollary

B.6
we obtain
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{2}_{r}
.
Therefore, by induction hypothesis, we have derivations
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi_{1}\triangleright J^{1}_{r}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{2}\triangleright J^{2}_{r}
.
We can derive the following
\Xi
.

\mathtt{(case)}

\Xi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
\Xi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}:\mathcal{E}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}):\mathcal{E}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Return

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})::\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
, so
\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\sqsubset\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
, therefore
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.
Let
J^{1}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{1}
, we set
J^{1}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
and have
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, we have also
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
.
Therfore, one can derive the following
\Xi
.

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Xi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Effect

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(eff)}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{N}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})::\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\}\!\!\}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}
, so
\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\}\!\!\}\sqsubset\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.
Let
J^{1}_{s}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusions of
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{2}
respectively. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exists contexts
\Gamma^{\prime},\Gamma_{i}\subseteq\Gamma
such that
\Gamma=\Gamma+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}
.
We set
J^{\prime}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{M}
and
J^{i}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}_{i}
for each
i\in I
. We have
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{\prime}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{i}_{r})
and by
Corollary

B.6
we obtain
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{\prime}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{i}_{r}
for each
i\in I
. We can then apply the induction hypothesis, obtaining derivations
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}\triangleright J^{\prime}_{r}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}\triangleright J^{2}_{r}
.
Therefore, we construct the following derivation
\Xi
.

\mathtt{(eff)}

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{M}
(\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}_{i})_{i\in I}
\Gamma^{\prime}+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Handler

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}::\mathsf{F}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{F}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
, so
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.
Let
J^{1}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{1}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{2}
. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exist contexts
\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\subseteq\Gamma
such that
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}
and a type
\mathcal{F}
such that
\mathsf{F}\sqsubset\mathcal{F}
. We set
J^{1}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
and
J^{2}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{F}
. We have
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{2}_{r})
and, by
Corollary

B.6
,
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{2}_{r}
.
We can then apply the induction hypothesis, obtaining derivations
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi_{1}\triangleright J^{1}_{r}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{2}\triangleright J^{2}_{r}
.
Therfore, one can derive the following
\Xi
.

\mathtt{(hdl)}

\Xi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
\Xi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{F}
\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Handler Branching Return

\Xi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(hdlr_{ret})}

\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;y::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}::\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
, therefore
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
,
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.
We can then construct a judgement
J^{1}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma;y:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
.
Let
J^{1}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{1}
we obtain
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
. Since
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, then also
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
. By induction hypothesis we obtain a derivation
\Xi_{1}
such that
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi_{1}
.
Therefore, one can derive the following
\Xi_{1}
:

\mathtt{(hdlr_{ret})}

\Xi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma;y:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}:\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case Handler Branching

\Pi
has the following form :

\mathtt{(hdlr)}

\Delta;y::\mathsf{M};r::\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{G}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}::\mathsf{F}\Rightarrow\mathsf{G}
\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}::\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{F}\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}

Being
J_{s}
the conclusion of
\Pi
,
J_{r}
is such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}\coloneq\Gamma+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}
, so
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
,
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}
,
\mathsf{N}\sqsubset\mathcal{N}_{i}
,
\mathsf{F}\sqsubset\mathcal{F}_{i}
for each
i\in I
and
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.
Let
J^{1}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{1}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi_{2}
. Since,
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
, there exist contexts
\Gamma^{\prime},\Gamma_{i}\subseteq\Gamma
and types
\mathcal{G}_{i}
such that
\mathsf{G}\sqsubset\mathcal{G}_{i}
. We set
J^{\prime}_{r}\coloneq\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t};y:\mathcal{M};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}

and
J^{i}_{r}\coloneq\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}
.
for each
i\in I
. We have
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{\prime}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{i}_{r})
and, by
Corollary

B.6
, we obtain
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{\prime}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{i}_{r}
for each
i\in I
. We can then apply the induction hypothesis, obtaining derivations
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}\triangleright J^{\prime}_{r}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}\triangleright J^{i}_{r}
for each
i\in I
.
Therefore, we construct the following derivation
\Xi
:

\mathtt{(hdlr)}

\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t};y:\mathcal{M};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
(\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}
(A)
\Gamma+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}

(A)=\begin{matrix}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\end{matrix}

Finally,
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

∎

Proposition B.22 (Completeness of
\mathcal{A}
).

Let
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\vdash p::\mathsf{T}
be a
\mathsf{HEB}
type derivation,
\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\vdash p:\tau
be an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type derivation such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
and
J_{r}
the conclusion of
\Xi
. Then
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Pi
.

Case Integers

\Pi
has the following form:

\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(int)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 6.75224pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$0<n\leq m$}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}:\mathsf{m}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\sqsubset\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(int)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 12.3056pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\mathcal{M}\in\{\left\{\,\right\},\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}\}$}}\hbox{$\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\underline{n}:\mathcal{M}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

It is clear that
J_{r}
respects the condition given by the defition of the function, therefore
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

Case Variable

\Pi
has the following form:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 45.43747pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(var)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{$\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}x::\mathsf{M}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\par}\par}}}$}&\sqsubset&\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 36.27078pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(var)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{$\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}x:\mathcal{M}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\par}\par}}}$}\end{array}

By definition of the refinement relation,
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
. Therefore,
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

Case Abstraction

\Pi
has the following form :

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(abs)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 12.79161pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}&\sqsubset&\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.04825pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(abs)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$(\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{M}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}_{i})_{i\in I}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\lambda x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\}_{i\in I}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\end{array}

With
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}
for each
i\in I
. Let
J^{\prime}_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi^{\prime}
and
J_{i}
the conclusion of
\Xi_{i}
, by
Lemma

B.7
, we have
J^{\prime}_{s}\sqsubset J_{i}
for each
i\in I
. Then, by induction hypothesis,
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi^{\prime},J_{i})
for each
i\in I
. Moreover, it obviously exists
\Gamma=+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}
, thus
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

Case Fixpoint

\Pi
has the following form :

\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(fix)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 10.40277pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}$}}\hbox{$\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\sqsubset\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{fix}\,x.{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathcal{M}

By
Lemma

B.15
there exists a context
\Gamma_{0}\subseteq\Gamma
and a simple path
\phi
between
(\Gamma_{0},\left\{\,\right\})
and
(\Gamma,\mathcal{M}^{\prime})
in
\mathcal{G}(\Pi^{\prime},\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E},\Gamma)
. Moreover,
Remark

B.14
there exist some
\mathsf{HEBI}
types
\mathcal{N}_{i}
for
i\leq|\phi|+1
such that
\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{N}_{i}
,
\mathcal{N}_{0}=\left\{\,\right\}
and
\mathcal{N}_{|\phi|+1}=\mathcal{M}
. Furthermore, there exist derivations
\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i};x:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{N}_{i+1}
for
i\leq|\phi|
such that
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}
and
\Gamma=+_{i\leq|\phi|}\Gamma_{i}
.
Let
J^{i}_{r}
be the conclusion of each
\Xi_{i}
and
J^{\prime}_{s}
be the conlcusion of
\Pi^{\prime}
, by
Lemma

B.7
, we have
J^{\prime}_{s}\sqsubset J^{i}_{r}
for each
i\leq n
. Therefore by induction hypothesis
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi^{\prime},J^{i}_{r})
for each
i\leq n
.
Finally, since
\phi
is simple,
Remark

B.13
implies
|\phi|\leq|\mathtt{Refn}(\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E})|\cdot|Sub(\Gamma)|
.
Taking
n=|\phi|
and
\Gamma=+_{i\leq n}\Gamma_{i}
, we can conclude
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

Case Application

\Pi
has the following form:

\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(app)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 15.4387pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{U}$}}\hbox{$\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{M}$}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}::\mathsf{U}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\sqsubset\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(app)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 4.0637pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathcal{N}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}\right\}$}}\hbox{$\Xi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{N}$}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mkern 1.0mu{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}w}:\mathcal{E}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

With
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi_{1}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{2}
. Let
J^{1}_{s}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusions of
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{2}
, moreover let
J^{1}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{r}
be the conclusions of
\Xi_{1},\Xi_{2}
. By
Lemma

B.7
, we have
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}
,
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
and
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
. By induction hypothesis
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
, thus
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.
Notice that
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{N}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{2}
, thus
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

Case Let-Binding

\Pi
has the following form :

\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(let)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 17.68762pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\mathsf{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{G}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\rightarrow}\mathsf{F}$}}\hbox{$\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{G}$}}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\mathsf{F}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\\[14.22636pt]
\sqsubset\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(let)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 15.81348pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\mathcal{E}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{j}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{j}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{j\in J}\;}}\mathcal{F}$}}\hbox{$(\Xi_{j}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{j};x:\mathcal{M}_{j}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{G}_{j})_{j\in J}$}}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}+_{j\in J}\Gamma_{j}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\end{array}

With
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}
for each
i\in I
. Let
J^{1}_{s}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusions of
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{2}
, moreover let
J^{\prime}_{r}
and
J^{j}_{r}
be the conclusions respectively of
\Xi^{\prime}
and
\Xi_{i}
. By
Lemma

B.7
, we have
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}
,
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{\prime}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{j}_{r}
. By induction hypothesis
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{\prime}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{j}_{r})
for each
j\in J
. Moreover, by
Lemma

B.18
,
|J|\leq|\mathcal{F}|
and by
Proposition

B.19
,
\models\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{M}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{M}_{|J|},\mathcal{G}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{G}_{|J|},\mathcal{F})
. Having
\Gamma=\Gamma^{\prime}+_{j\in J}\Gamma_{j}
, all the conditions in the definition of
\mathcal{A}
are satisfied, thus
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

Case Case

\Pi
has the following form :

\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(case)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 5.7912pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{n}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$(\Pi_{k}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{k}::\mathsf{U})_{0<k\leq n}$}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n})::\mathsf{U}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\\[14.22636pt]
\sqsubset\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 11.8813pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(case)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$0<m\leq n$}}\hbox{$\Xi_{m}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{m}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{m}:\mathcal{E}$}}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{m}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{case}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{n}):\mathcal{E}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\end{array}

With
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi_{1}
and
\Pi_{m}\sqsubset\Xi_{m}
. Let
J^{1}_{s}
and
J^{m}_{s}
be the conclusions of
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{m}
, moreover let
J^{m}_{r}
and
J^{m}_{r}
be the conclusions of
\Xi_{1},\Xi_{m}
. By
Lemma

B.7
, we have
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}
,
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{1}_{r}
and
J^{m}_{s}\sqsubset J^{m}_{r}
. By induction hypothesis
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{1}_{r})
. Since
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}+\Gamma_{m}
and
\mathsf{n}\sqsubset\left\{\mathsf{m}\right\}
, we conclude
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

has the following form :

\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 38.08398pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(hdlr_{\sigma})}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;y::\mathsf{M};r::\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{G}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{E}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}::\mathsf{F}\Rightarrow\mathsf{G}$}}\hbox{$\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}$}}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}::\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{F}\}\!\!\}\Rightarrow\mathsf{E}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\\[14.22636pt]
\sqsubset\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 63.65514pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(hdlr_{\sigma})}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma^{\prime};y:\mathcal{M};r:\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i\in I}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$(\Xi_{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\mathcal{F}_{i}\Rightarrow\mathcal{G}_{i})_{i\in I}$}}\hbox{$\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\mathrel{\#}{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}$}}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\sigma(y;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}:\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}\Rightarrow\mathcal{E}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\end{array}

With
\Pi_{1}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
and
\Pi_{2}\sqsubset\Xi_{i}
for each
i\in I
. Let
J^{1}_{s}
and
J^{2}_{s}
be the conclusions of
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{2}
, moreover let
J^{\prime}_{r}
and
J^{i}_{r}
be the conclusions respectively of
\Xi^{\prime}
and
\Xi_{i}
. By
Lemma

B.7
, we have
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{r}
,
J^{1}_{s}\sqsubset J^{\prime}_{r}
and
J^{2}_{s}\sqsubset J^{i}_{r}
. By induction hypothesis
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{1},J^{\prime}_{r})
and
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi_{2},J^{i}_{r})
. Having
\Gamma=\Gamma^{\prime}+_{i\in I}\Gamma_{i}
and
\mathsf{G}\sqsubset\mathcal{G}_{i}
for all
i\in I
, we conclude
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{r})
.

∎

In order to prove the computibility of
\mathcal{A}
, we must show that all quantifiers appearing in its definition range over finite sets. The following lemmas establish this property for the sets of refinements
\mathtt{Refn}(\mathsf{T})
and of subcontexts
Sub(\Gamma)
, where
\mathsf{T}
is an
\mathsf{HEB}
type and
\Gamma
is an
\mathsf{HEBI}
context.

See
5.4

Proof.

By induction on
\mathsf{T}
.

Case Integers

By looking at
Fig.

9
, in case
\mathsf{T}=\mathsf{m}
we have

\begin{array}[]{cc}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.86113pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{$\mathsf{m}\sqsubset\left\{\,\right\}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\par}\par}}}$}&\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hskip 11.94666pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$0<n\leq m\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\mathsf{m}\sqsubset\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\end{array}

The number of possible refinement is then finite.

Case Arrow

If
\mathsf{T}=\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}
, then

\left(\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}\right)_{i\in I}
\left(\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}_{i}\right)_{i\in I}
\mathsf{M}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}

By induction hypothesis, there exists a finite number of
\mathsf{HEBI}
types
\mathcal{N}_{i}
and
\mathcal{E}_{i}
such that
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}_{i}
and
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}_{i}
. Therefore, there are only finitely many possible arrow types
\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}
. Thus, the number of types of the form
\left\{\mathcal{M}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\right\}_{i\in I}
is finite.

Case Computation Effect

If
\mathsf{T}=\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\}\!\!\}
. We have

\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
\left(\mathsf{N}\sqsubset\mathcal{N}_{i}\right)_{i\in I}
\left(\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}_{i}\right)_{i\in I}
\prescript{\sigma[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathsf{N}\rightarrow\mathsf{E}\}\!\!\}\sqsubset\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

As before by induction hypothesis, the number of
\mathsf{HEBI}
types such that
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
,
\mathsf{N}\sqsubset\mathcal{N}_{i}
and
\mathsf{E}\sqsubset\mathcal{E}_{i}
are finite. Therefore, there are finitely many types of the form
\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}
.

The other two cases (
\mathsf{T}=\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
and
\mathsf{T}=\mathsf{E}\Rightarrow\mathsf{F}
) are similar.
∎

Corollary B.23 (Finite Refinement for Contexts).

Let
\Delta
be an
\mathsf{HEB}
contex. There exists a finite number of
\mathsf{HEBI}
contexts
\Gamma
such that
\Delta\sqsubset\Gamma
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Delta
.
∎

Lemma B.24 (Finite Subsets).

Let
\mathcal{M}
an
\mathsf{HEBI}
value type and
\Gamma
be an
\mathsf{HEBI}
typing context:

•

The types
\mathcal{N}
such that
\mathcal{N}\subseteq\mathcal{M}
are finite.

•

The contexts
\Gamma^{\prime}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}\subseteq\Gamma
are finite.

See
5.6

Proof.

The equality checks perfomed in base cases are clearly computable. In particular, in the
\mathtt{(var)}

case, we have to check whether
\mathsf{M}\sqsubset\mathcal{M}
holds, given an
\mathsf{HEB}
type
\mathsf{M}
and an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\mathcal{M}
. The relation
\sqsubset
between these types is defined inductively over the structure of the types, and is clearly computable.
In most inductive cases, such as
\mathtt{(app)}

,
\mathtt{(fix)}

or
\mathtt{(let)}

, there is an existential quantification over subcontexts of a given
\mathsf{HEBI}
context
\Gamma
.
Lemma

B.24
ensures that the set
Sub(\Gamma)
is finite. In several similar cases, there is an existential quantification over all possible refinements of a given
\mathsf{HEB}
type. By
Lemma

5.4
, the set of such refinements is finite.
In the case of
\mathtt{(fix)}

, we search for an integer
n
, bounded by
|\mathtt{Refn}(\mathsf{M})|\cdot|Sub(\Gamma)|
, for some
\mathsf{HEB}
type
\mathsf{M}
and some
\mathsf{HEBI}
typing context
\Gamma
. As we already noticed, both these numbers are finite for each
\mathsf{M}
and
\Gamma
.
Similarly, in the
\mathtt{(let)}

case, the existential quantification is over a set
I
whose size is bounded by
|\mathcal{F}|
, for some
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\mathcal{F}
. According to
Remark

B.17
, this measure is finite for each
\mathcal{F}
. Finally, we use the algorithm
\mathcal{B}
, whose computability is established in
Lemma

B.20
. Therefore, all quantifiers appearing in
Figs.

12
and
18
range over finite sets. We can conclude that there exists an algorithm that given an
\mathsf{HEB}
derivation
\Pi
and an
\mathsf{HEBI}
judgement
J
determines wheter
\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J)
holds.
∎

See
5.7

Proof.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
be a closed term typable with
\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{m}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
in
\mathsf{HEB}
, there exists an
\mathsf{HEB}
derivation
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{m}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
. By
Lemma

5.4
, there are only finitely many
\mathsf{HEBI}
types
\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}
such that
\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathsf{m}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\sqsubset\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
, in particular one for each
0<n\leq m
. Let
J_{s}
be the conclusion of
\Pi
, we can then construct a finite amount of
\mathsf{HEBI}
judgements
J_{n}\coloneq\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
such that
J_{s}\sqsubset J_{n}
. If
\models\mathcal{A}(\Pi,J_{n}
) for any of those, by
Proposition

B.21

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\left\{\mathsf{n}\right\}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}
, and
Theorem

4.11
implies
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\twoheadrightarrow{}{}\mathsf{return}(\underline{n})
. Finally, this process is decidable because of
Proposition

5.6
.
∎

Appendix C
Proofs of Section
6

We omit here the full proof of Subject Reduction for
\HEPCF
. However, we state the transformations induced by the reduction on terms, as we did for
\mathsf{HEB}
. The proof proceeds using standard techniques, in which the derivation of
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is constructed according to the transformations described in
Fig.

21
. Since several of the transformation rules for
\HEPCF
coincide with those for
\mathsf{HEB}
, we only present those that differ.

Theorem C.1 (
\HEPCF
Subject Reduction).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
,
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{U}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
then it exists
\Pi^{\prime}
such that
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}::\mathsf{U}
.

To obtain the full subject reduction for
\HEPCF
, we need to prove the following lemma.

Lemma C.2 (
\HEPCF
Context Typing).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
and

\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
a computation context such that

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>::\mathsf{U}
.
Then there is an
\mathsf{HEB}
type
\mathsf{S}

such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{S}
and
for every
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathsf{S}
, then there exist
\Pi^{\prime}
such that

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>::\mathsf{U}
.

A Progress Lemma can also be proved in the exact same way as we already did in
Lemma

4.7
.

Lemma C.3 (
\HEPCF
, Progress).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
be a closed term such that
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{U}
. Then either there exists
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}
, or
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
is a normal form computation, that is, either
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}=\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})
or
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}=\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})
for some
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\texttt{Val}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
.

The proof of Relative Subject Expansion for
\HEPCF
follows the same strategy already used in the case of
\mathsf{HEB}
, in particular relying on the definitions of refinement for types and derivations, as well as the notions of reduction and substitution between derivations. Due to these similarities, we omit the full proofs. We also observe that all lemmas concerning refinements for subsets and contexts stated in
Section

B.2
remain valid in essentially the same form for
\HEPCF
, since they only depend on value types, which coincide in the two systems.

In
Figs.

19
and
20
, we present the refinement relations between
\mathsf{HEBI}
and
\HEPCF
derivations. Again, given that several rules of
\HEPCF
are identical to those of
\mathsf{HEB}
, we only present those that differ.

\begin{array}[]{c}\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(let)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 9.7015pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Pi_{1}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\prescript{}{\mathtt{E}}{\mathsf{M}}\hfil\lx@intercol$}}\hbox{$\Pi_{2}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\prescript{}{\mathtt{E}}{\mathsf{N}}$}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}::\prescript{}{\mathtt{E}}{\mathsf{N}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}\\[14.22636pt]
