Figure 21.
\HEPCF
, Reduction Rules.

Proposition C.4 (
\HEPCF
Weakening Refinement).

Let
p
be a term,
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta_{1}\vdash p::\mathsf{U}
be its derivation and
\Delta_{2}
be a context such that for each
x::\mathsf{M}\in\Delta_{1}
and
x::\mathsf{N}\in\Delta_{2}
then
\mathsf{M}=\mathsf{N}
. Let
\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\vdash p:\mathcal{E}
be an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type derivation, then
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
if and only if
\Pi^{(\Delta_{2})}\sqsubset\Xi
.

Lemma C.5 (
\HEPCF
Anti-substitution Lemma).

Let
p\in\Lambda

be any term,
x
a variable occourring free in
p
and
{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\texttt{Val}}}
a closed value.
If
\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\vdash p\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\tau
for some
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\tau
and there exists an
\HEPCF
type
\mathsf{M}
such that
\Pi_{p}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Delta;x::\mathsf{M}\vdash p::\mathsf{T}
,
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\vdash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
and
\Pi_{p}\{x\backslash\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\sqsubset\Pi
, then it exists an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\mathcal{M}
such that

\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
and

\Pi_{p}\sqsubset\Xi_{p}\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma;x:\mathcal{M}\vdash p:\tau
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Xi
.
∎

Lemma C.6 (Relative Elementary Subject Expansion for
\HEPCF
).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
be two closed term such that

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\mapsto{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. If there exists

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}
and
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}::\mathsf{U}
such that
\Pi^{\prime}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}
and also there exist
\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{U}
such that
\Pi\rightsquigarrow\Pi^{\prime}
,
then there exists

\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}:\mathcal{E}
such that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Proof.

By case analysis on the different rewrite rules. We are going to detail only the new rewriting rules, the other cases being identical to
Lemma

5.3

Case
\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ret}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ret}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By
definition,
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
. Since
t
is simply typable, we get the existence of two derivations
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}::\mathsf{M}
and
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\mathrel{\triangleright}x::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}u::\prescript{}{\mathtt{E}}{\mathsf{N}}
for some
\mathsf{M}
. Also,
there exist derivations
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}::\prescript{}{\mathtt{E}}{\mathsf{M}}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{F}
.
Since by hypothesis
\Pi\rightsquigarrow\Pi^{\prime}
, by definition of
\rightsquigarrow
we have that
\Pi^{\prime}=\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}
.
By anti-substitution lemma (
Lemma

C.5
), there exist a refinement type
\mathcal{M}

and derivations
\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}

and
\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
,
where
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}
and
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
.
Thus, let
\Xi
be the following derivation :

\mathtt{(let)}

\mathtt{(ret)}

\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}V}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}:\mathcal{M}
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}):\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}

\prescript{\mathsf{return}[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\,\}\!\!\}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\rightarrow}\mathcal{F}

\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}
\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}:\mathcal{F}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}

Finally, we can see that
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

Case
\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ef}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{let\mbox{-}ef}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
. By
definition,
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s})\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
and

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t})
. There exists a derivation

\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}):\mathcal{E}

for some type
\mathcal{E}
. By
case analysis, we deduce that
\Xi^{\prime}
is necessarily of the following
form:

\mathtt{(eff)}

\left(\vrule height=7.5pt,depth=1.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.

\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}):\prescript{\sigma[\mathcal{M}]\!}{}{\{}\!\!\{\mathcal{N}_{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{i}\}\!\!\}_{i\in I}

(A_{i})\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\enskip\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\text{$\mathtt{(let)}$ }$}}}\hbox{\hskip 19.58656pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=8.26085pt,depth=1.94444pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\Xi^{i}_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}}\mathrel{\triangleright}y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}:\mathcal{G}_{i}$}}\hbox{$B_{i}$}}}$}\right)_{i\in I}$}}}\hbox{$(\Xi_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\triangleright}x:\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{F}_{k}^{i})_{k\in K_{i}}$}}}}}\hbox{$y:\mathcal{N}_{i}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{E}_{i}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

(B_{i})\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.47906pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\enskip\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\varphi_{i}$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to10.33325pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\mathcal{G}_{i}\mathrel{-\>\!\!\!\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_{k}^{i}\rightarrow\mathcal{F}_{k}^{i}\right\}\>\!\!\!\!\overrightarrow{\,\vskip-8.69995pt{}^{k\in K_{i}}\;}}\mathcal{E}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Since

is not a free variable of

.
Furthermore, it exists a
\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\sigma({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};y.\mathsf{let}\,x\doteq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}s}\,\mathsf{in}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})::\prescript{}{\mathtt{E}}{\mathsf{M}^{\prime}}
.
Let

\mathtt{(let)}

(A)
(B)

By looking at the definition of this reduction case in

, from

we obtain easily that

. In particular, notice that the subderivation

,

. By

.

Case
\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ret}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that

. By

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\mathsf{return}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v})
and

with

Since

\mathtt{(hdl)}

Case
\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ef}
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t},{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
is
typed and
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\rightarrow_{\mathtt{hdl\mbox{-}ef}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}
.

By definition,

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\coloneqq\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,\sigma_{k}({\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v};x.{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u})

{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}^{\prime}\coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{k}\{x\backslash{\color[rgb]{0.9688,0.4766,0}v}\}\{r\backslash\lambda y.\mathsf{with}\,{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\,\mathsf{handle}\,{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\}

with

\begin{matrix}y::\mathsf{M}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{U}\\
\{\sigma_{j}:{\mathsf{M}^{\prime}_{j}}\rightsquigarrow{\mathsf{N}_{j}}\in\mathsf{E}\;|\;1\leq j\leq n\}\end{matrix}
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}H}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\{\mathsf{return}(y)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\}\cup\{\sigma_{\mkern-2.0muj}(x;r)\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}_{j}\;|\;1\leq j\leq n\}::\prescript{}{\mathtt{E}}{\mathsf{M}}\Rightarrow\mathsf{U}

We have
\Pi_{k}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}
and
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\sqsubset\Xi^{i}_{{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}}
for each
i\in I
, so
\Pi_{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}\sqsubset\Xi_{\color[rgb]{0.6992,0,1}h}
.
One can conclude this case by noticing that

\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\Gamma\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}t:\mathcal{E}
and
\Pi\sqsubset\Xi
.

∎

As usual, to obtain
Lemma

6.1
, we need the following lemma.

Lemma C.7 (
\HEPCF
Refinement Context Typing).

Let
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\in\Lambda_{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\texttt{Cmp}}}
and

\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
a computation context such that

\Pi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>::\mathsf{U}\sqsubset\Xi\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}\right>:\mathcal{U}
.
Then there exists an
\mathsf{HEBI}
type
\mathcal{S}
and an
\HEPCF
type
\mathsf{S}

such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathsf{S}\sqsubset\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}t}::\mathcal{F}
and
for every
{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}
such that
\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathsf{S}\sqsubset\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}:\mathcal{S}
, then there exist
\Pi^{\prime}
and
\Xi^{\prime}
such that

\Pi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathsf{U}\sqsubset\Xi^{\prime}\mathrel{\triangleright}\emptyset\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 4.44441pt\hskip-4.44441pt\hbox{${\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}\vdash}$}\hskip-4.44441pt\hskip-1.86803pt\raisebox{7.44444pt}{\hbox{$\mkern 2.0mu{\scriptscriptstyle\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}C}}}$}}\hskip-1.86803pt\hskip 4.44441pt}}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}\!\left<{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}u}\right>:\mathcal{U}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.4805,0.7773,0.0977}E}}
.
∎
